     return 'SLA Breach, Missing Escalation'\n    elif sb == 1:\n        return 'SLA Breach'\n    elif me == 1:\n        return 'Missing Escalation'\n    else:\n        return 'None'\n\nformatted['Error Summary'] = formatted.apply(error_summary, axis=1)\n\n# ---- Summary sheet ----\n# Aggregate by (Queue, Region)\nsummary = formatted.groupby(['Queue', 'Region'],
